# Skill Gaps Log

Append-only record of ZK features that the harness missed and how we structurally caught them. See `doc/skill-feedback-loop.md` for the workflow.

## Columns

- **date**: ISO date the gap was identified
- **component(s)**: which component(s) the gap affects (comma-separated when shared)
- **gap**: one sentence — what was wrong
- **why-harness-missed-it**: one sentence — why the existing process didn't catch it
- **layer**: `skill` / `contract` / `agent` (or combination, e.g. `skill+agent`)
- **fix-location**: relative paths or section refs where the fix landed
- **retrospective**: one sentence after the fact — did the layered fix catch siblings, or did more gaps surface?

## Log

| date | component(s) | gap | why-harness-missed-it | layer | fix-location | retrospective |

| 2026-07-02 | button | Filled/contained button hover was under-built: only an 8% white state-layer overlay (`--zk-state-hover-opacity`) with **no elevation change**, so the hover reads as almost no change (user reported "差距不大"). MD3 filled buttons raise elevation 0→1 on hover *in addition to* the state layer, and MUI (this theme's stated visual target) darkens the background too — the theme did neither. | Contract c11 described the hover as "darker tint of c8 (state layer 0.08)" — self-contradictory, because the overlay color is white/on-primary and thus *lightens* on a blue button, not darkens; and no row ever asserted a hover `box-shadow`/elevation delta, so the evaluator only measured overlay opacity and nothing flagged the missing lift. No MD3-vs-MUI reconciliation existed for the hover, so the softest possible reading (overlay only) shipped. | contract | CSS `js/zul/wgt/css/button.css` — filled-scoped `:hover` rule adds `box-shadow: var(--zk-elevation-2)` + bumps overlay opacity to 0.12, excluding outlined/text/icon/FAB/disabled via `:not([class*=…])`. Contract `doc/contracts/button.md` — corrected c11 wording (white/on-primary state layer ~0.12, **lightens** not darkens) + new c29 (`.z-button:hover` box-shadow == elevation-2 for filled/contained; flat & FAB variants unchanged). Decision-rule: theme-design (fails survives-theme-rewrite test) → contract layer, no skill change. | 2026-07-02 verified live on /button.zul via Playwright hover probe: contained (`.z-button-secondary`) and filled-default box-shadow go resting→`--zk-elevation-2` on hover with overlay 0→0.12; outlined stays `box-shadow: none` and keeps the canonical 0.08 overlay (exclusion selector works). Image swap confirmed (both `<img>` src = DisketteBlack). |
| 2026-06-29 | colorbox | The picker CSS reached into zkex.jar's sprite assets via hardcoded `/zkau/web/zkex/img/colorbox/*.{png,gif}` URLs (a DSP-free rewrite of stock's `${c:encodeURL(...)}`), creating a hidden coupling to zkex's asset layout and breaking under any non-root servlet context-path — the absolute URL omits the context path that `encodeURL` would prepend (it returns early via the `~.` ExtendletContext branch in `Encodes.encodeURL0`). | The evaluator only checks computed styles on the root-context preview server, where `/zkau/web/...` happens to resolve; it never verifies resource-URL robustness (context-path / CDN / version stamp) nor flags cross-jar asset dependencies, so the latent breakage was invisible. | impl + skill-note | CSS `js/zkex/inp/css/colorbox.css`: SV square + hue bar redrawn as CSS gradients (`.z-colorpicker-overlay` two-layer white/black, `.z-colorpicker-bar` 360°→0° spectrum), the two marker GIFs inlined as data URIs (`.z-colorpicker-circle` / `-arrows`); 0 external image refs. JS-orthogonality: `Colorpicker.ts` only sets overlay `background-color` (hue tint) + marker `top/left`, never `background-image`; gradients aligned to its `sat=x/255` / `val=1−y/255` / `hue=(256−y)×359.99/255`. | Verified via Playwright probe (computed bg = gradients/data URIs, `overlayColor=rgb(255,0,0)` confirms the JS hue tint still drives the SV square) + screenshot. Reusable for any EE component hardcoding zkex/zkmax sprite URLs — prefer CSS gradients for gradient sprites + data URIs for tiny markers to stay DSP-free, context-path-safe, and independent of the source jar's asset layout. |

| 2026-05-14 | combobox | `buttonVisible="false"` hides the button but the right edge of the input has no border and corner radius collapses (input has `border-right: none` + `border-radius: R 0 0 R`, expecting the button to provide the right edge). ZK already emits `z-combobox-input-full` class on the input when buttonVisible=false — the rule to restore right border + full radius is the missing piece. | The previous gap (No-button cell) only checked that the button is hidden. It did NOT check that the remaining input still looks correct. Generic "buttonVisible-false" check ignored that combobox uses a SPLIT-BORDER pattern (input border-right: none, button provides right edge) whereas bandbox/datebox/timebox/spinner use ROOT-BORDER (border on `.z-{c}`, input `border: none`) so hiding the button doesn't break their borders. | skill + contract + agent | Skill: `reference/buttonVisible-attribute.md` "Border architecture: split-border vs root-border" section + the `z-{c}-input-full` restoration hook; Contract: `combobox.md` No-button check split into sub-checks (a) button hidden, (b) input has 4-side border + uniform radius; Agent: evaluator §1.5 buttonVisible-false trigger now requires reading border-right-style and four border-radius values for Pattern S components | 2026-05-14 fix complete (combobox.css: added `.z-combobox-input.z-combobox-input-full { border-right: 1px solid …; border-radius: …; }`). All 5 No-button cells verified: border-right `1px solid` not `none`, border-radius `4px` uniform; per-state cascade preserved (default/disabled/readonly/invalid/inplace all colour all 4 sides correctly). |

| 2026-05-14 | chosenbox | Focus 後 popup 寬度撐滿整個 viewport (1904px) 而非 chosenbox 自己的 200px。CSS 用 `.z-chosenbox-popup { min-width: 100%; }`，但 ZK 把該 popup 掛在 `<body>` 之下（不是掛在 chosenbox 內），所以 `100%` 解析成 body 寬度，蓋掉了 ZK 已 inline 設好的 `width: 200px`。 | Skill 沒有記錄「ZK floating popup 會脫離 trigger，掛到 body」這個 ZK 行為；contract 也沒有檢查「popup 算出來的寬度應等於 trigger 寬度」。先前 combobox/datebox 的 popup 沒踩到這雷只是因為它們的 CSS 沒寫 `width: 100%` / `min-width: 100%`。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill: 新增 `reference/floating-popup-in-body.md`（描述 ZK 把 popup 掛到 body 的渲染模式 + percentage-based width 的陷阱 + ZK 已設 inline width 因此 CSS 不該再加 width hint）；Contract: `chosenbox.md` 改正 DOM 選擇器（`.z-chosenbox-item` 非 `-tag`、`.z-chosenbox-option` 非 `-item`），新增 popup 寬度檢查（rendered width == trigger width）；Agent: evaluator §1.5 新增「popup-floating 寬度核對」步驟 | 2026-05-14 fix complete (chosenbox.css 移除 `.z-chosenbox-popup` 的 `min-width: 100%`)：focus 後 popup width = 200px，與 trigger 同寬。 |

| 2026-06-10 | multislider, slider | Vertical thumbs sit 10px below the track endpoints (sweep found the same bug in the plain `.z-slider-vertical .z-slider-button`): JS writes `btn.style.top = X%` (the value position, meant for the thumb CENTER), but `.z-multislider-vertical .z-sliderbuttons-button` set `margin-top: 0`, so the thumb's TOP EDGE — not its center — lands on the position. Every thumb center is off by +half-height (10px). The rangeslider sibling does it right (`margin-top: calc(--btn-size / -2)`). User: 圓心沒對齊豎線起點/終點，兩者都偏移. | The harness never measured vertical thumb-vs-track-endpoint alignment. multislider.md contract listed phantom selectors (`.z-multislider-center/-area/-button` — none exist in live DOM; real DOM is `.z-multislider-track`, `.z-sliderbuttons-area`, `.z-sliderbuttons-button`) and only checked colors/size, never geometry. The main-axis centering rule was implicit in horizontal (`margin-left:-10px`) and correct in rangeslider, but nobody asserted it for multislider vertical. | skill + contract | Skill: `components/slider.md` "Related: multislider, rangeslider" — new "Thumb must be centered on its JS-set offset" rule (negative margin = −½ thumb size on the JS-driven axis; applies to slider/rangeslider/multislider, both orientations). Contract: `doc/contracts/multislider.md` selectors corrected to real DOM + new failing-first assertion v1 (vertical thumb center y == track endpoint y). CSS: `multislider.css` + `slider.css` (`.z-slider-vertical .z-slider-button`) `margin-top: -10px`. | 2026-06-10 fix: after `margin-top:-10px`, live re-measure shows multislider start thumb (value 5) center == track top (343px) and all thumb centers align to their value offsets (was +10px each); vertical filled-area edges now coincide with thumb centers. Step-5 sweep found the identical `margin-top:0` bug in the plain vertical slider (offset +10px → 0px after same fix). rangeslider already correct. Horizontal unaffected (uses base `margin-top:-10px`). |

CSS: `rangeslider.css` adds `--zk-rangeslider-label-gap: 18px` token; all four label offsets = `calc(var(--gap) + correction)` (rangeslider +4, multislider +3 H / +5 V). | 2026-06-11 verified live: all four cases measure exactly 18px (was 27/15/10/18). | 2026-06-23 | grid/listbox (frozen), drag-and-drop widgets | 6 JS-toggled global framework classes undefined in Marble: `.z-word-nowrap` (Frozen.ts frozen columns), `.z-drag-over`/`.z-drop-allow`/`.z-drop-disallow` (DnD drop-target feedback), `.z-dragged`/`.z-drag-ghost` (drag source/ghost). Marble replaces stock CSS so they are simply absent. | The harness is per-component and driven by MD3/Mira analogs; these are cross-cutting classes ZK's client JS toggles at runtime, with no design-reference analog, so no contract ever asserted them. Same root cause as the splitlayout `.z-flex` rename bug, generalised. | skill + tooling | Spec: `reference/framework-classes.md` + `framework-classes.json` (canonical registry, contract-vs-decorative). Tooling: `tools/check-framework-classes.mjs` (Tier-1 presence checker, CI-gateable; re-run for the live PASS/FAIL result). | Built a reusable two-tier compliance check (script + semantic-review procedure) that any ZK theme can run; `.z-flex` family confirmed compliant; the 6 DnD/frozen gaps are logged for scoped follow-up (F1 frozen → F2 drop feedback → F3 drag ghost). **RESOLVED 2026-06-23 via TDD**: building the fix surfaced the registry was incomplete — the listitem message-ghost adds `.z-drop-ghost`/`-content`/`-icon`/`-text` (not in the 12), and the 5 drag hooks have NO stock CSS (registry wrongly said norm.less). Registry corrected/extended to 16 contract classes; all implemented in `zul/css/base/_dnd.css`. Preview `dnd.zul`, tests `framework-classes.spec.ts` (7/7), contract `doc/contracts/framework-classes.md`, checker PASS 16/16. Lesson: a JS-grep registry can miss sibling classes in the same DOM subtree — actually rendering/building the feature is the completeness check. |

| 2026-05-14 | colorbox | 預設關閉狀態結構壞掉：root 寬度只剩 ~18px（沒設定寬高、`overflow: hidden`），`.z-colorbox-current` swatch 因 inline-block 沒 width/height 規則被壓成 3.6×20，使用者看不到選定的顏色；caret-down 圖示沒有 mask SVG 完全空白。原本期望是「一個方形框框，內含色塊 + 向下箭頭」。 | Contract 寫了錯誤 DOM 選擇器（沒有 `.z-colorbox-current`、把 `.z-colorbox-picker` 當 popup 但實際是 `.z-colorbox-popup` 包 `.z-colorpicker`/`.z-colorpalette`），所以 expected-values 沒覆蓋到真正的 swatch；Skill 也沒有 `components/colorbox.md` 紀錄 mold 渲染出來的真正結構（colorbox.js `current` + `button` + `popup`(picker+palette) 三層），生成 CSS 時只憑想像寫了 `.z-colorbox` + `.z-colorbox-button` 兩條規則。 | skill + contract | Skill: 新增 `components/colorbox.md`（記錄 mold 渲染的 DOM、`.z-colorbox-current` 由 ZK inline `style="background-color"` 帶顏色、`.z-icon-caret-down` 需要本地 mask、popup 內部有 `.z-colorpicker`/`.z-colorpalette` 兩種子元件、EE-only）；Contract: 改正 `colorbox.md` 全部選擇器與 expected-values，加 swatch 尺寸 / caret 渲染 / popup 寬度檢查 | 2026-05-14 fix complete (colorbox.css 重寫關閉狀態：root 設 32×32 inline-flex、`.z-colorbox-current` flex:1 顯示色塊、`.z-colorbox-button` 固定寬度 16px、`.z-colorbox-icon` 本地 mask 出 chevron-down)。Live DOM：root width = 32, swatch width = 14, button width = 16, icon mask 渲染 ok。Popup 設計另開 round 處理（picker / palette 內部用 ZK 既有像素座標，無法套 MD3）。 |

| 2026-05-14 | colorbox (但 root cause 影響所有 css.dsp 檔) | colorbox popup 內部完全沒樣式：palette 縮成 1px、picker 看不到 gradient。Root cause: 我在 colorbox.css 寫了原 ZK 主題慣用的 `${c:encodeURL("~./zkex/img/...")}` URL 語法 — 但我們的 build pipeline 只把 `.css` 改成 `.css.dsp` 副檔名，沒在檔首加 `<%@ taglib %>` declaration。DSP 處理時找不到 `c:` taglib → 處理失敗 → 檔案內容變空 → WCS bundler 整檔丟掉，連無 EL 表達式的部分也跟著消失。 | Skill 沒有記錄「我們的 .css.dsp 沒走 DSP processing，不能用 `${...}` 表達式」這個 pipeline 事實。任何 css.dsp 檔只要用了 EL/JSP 標籤都會被默默掃光，沒有錯誤訊息、build 也 pass。一個檔內只要一個 `${c:encodeURL(...)}` 就把整個檔抹掉。 | skill + contract | Skill: 新增 `reference/css-dsp-pipeline.md` 記 build 只 rename 不 process EL；外部 URL 用絕對路徑 `/zkau/web/zkex/img/...`（ZK 無 version 也吃）；想用 `${...}` 需要先加 taglib 並讓 build 串接 DSP；Contract: `colorbox.md` 加 picker sprite URL 期望值（status 200、resolved URL contains `/zkau/web/zkex/img/`） | 2026-05-14 fix complete (colorbox.css 把 `${c:encodeURL("~./zkex/img/...")}` 改成 `/zkau/web/zkex/img/...`)。WCS 重新拿到 73 條 colorbox 規則（原本 0），sprite 4 張 status 200，picker 跟 palette 都正常渲染。Closed state 仍 40×36，popup 358×318 含 8px padding + border。 |

| 2026-05-14 | combobutton (toolbar mold) | 4 個角落顯示 border 缺口。Root cause：root `.z-combobutton-toolbar` 有 `border-radius + overflow: hidden`，border 卻畫在 child `.z-combobutton-content` 上 — child 的方形 border 在邊角被 root 的 rounded clip 切掉。 | Skill 沒有「同一元素必須擁有 border + border-radius 才能畫出連續圓角」的條目；contract 沒檢查 root vs inner 哪邊有 border。 | component CSS | combobutton.css: 把 border 從 `.z-combobutton-content` 搬到 root `.z-combobutton-toolbar` | 2026-05-14 fix complete: 角落連續，4 個角無缺口。 |
| 2026-05-14 | datebox popup | popup 右邊有 12px 寬的多餘區塊，像是「多一層 border」。Root cause：`.z-datebox-popup { min-width: 280px }` 強制 popup 280 寬，但裡面 `.z-calendar` 有自己的 `max-width: 268px` cap，結果右側 12px 是 popup 的空白背景條。 | 兩個檔（datebox.css 跟 calendar.css）各自設寬度，沒有 cross-file 對齊檢查；contract 沒檢查 popup width vs calendar width 是否吻合。 | component CSS | datebox.css: 移除 `.z-datebox-popup { min-width: 280px }` 跟 `.z-datebox-popup .z-calendar { width: 320px }`（讓 popup auto-fit calendar 的 max-width） | 2026-05-14 fix complete: popupW=calW=268, diff=0。 |
| 2026-05-14 | rangeslider (3 sub-issues) | (a) marks 垂直堆疊不是水平排列；(b) vertical track 完全沒畫；(c) 我們的 rangeslider.css 只給 CSS variables 沒給結構規則，整個 mark layout / orientation / button geometry 全部使用 ZK stock default，但 stock 的 padding/margin/size 跟 MD3 token 不一致。 | 我們的 rangeslider.css 是「stock CSS 的 token-only 替身」 — 沒意識到 ZK theme override 是「整檔取代」，不是「附加」。覆蓋了 stock 卻沒搬進去結構規則，導致 marks 變 static block、vertical 沒尺寸。Skill 沒有警告 theme override 是 replace 不是 merge。 | skill + component CSS | Skill (待續): `reference/theme-override-is-replace.md` 記 lang-addon `<css-uri>` 路徑被 theme 整檔取代；component CSS: 重寫 `js/zkex/slider/css/rangeslider.css` 包含 marks、horizontal/vertical、track、area、button、tooltip 結構規則（搬自 stock 並用 `--zk-*` token） | 2026-05-14 fix complete: 水平 inner 500×4 marks position:absolute、垂直 inner 4×500 track 渲染、button 20×20 radius 50%。 |
| 2026-05-14 | multislider vertical | vertical multislider 的 track 高度 0，看不到。Root cause：`.z-multislider-vertical .z-multislider-track { width: 4px; height: 100% }` 中的 100% 算的是 parent `.z-multislider-inner` 的高度 — 但 inner 沒設高度（預設 0），所以 track 0 高。 | Contract 沒檢查 vertical inner 是否能撐滿 parent height；horizontal 沒這個問題（inner 是 block 自然撐滿寬度），vertical 才有 — orientation-specific bug contract 沒涵蓋。 | component CSS | multislider.css: 加 `.z-multislider-vertical .z-multislider-inner { width: 4px; height: 100%; margin: 12px 30px 12px 12px }`（跟 rangeslider 同 pattern） | 2026-05-14 fix complete: vertical inner 4×500, track 4×500, 完整渲染。 |

| 2026-05-14 | combobutton popup（其實牽動所有 ZK 用 `<a>` 渲染的 widget UI） | popup item hover 出現底線。Root cause：`base/_reset.css` 寫的 `a:hover { text-decoration: underline }` 是無 scope 的全域 reset，吃到所有 `<a>` 元素。但 ZK 用 `<a>` 渲染很多 UI 控件（`.z-menuitem-content`、`.z-toolbarbutton`、`.z-tab`、`.z-listitem`、`.z-treerow`、`.z-a`…），底線跑到那些控件 hover 時看起來像 bug。 | Skill 沒紀錄「ZK 大量用 `<a>` 渲染非超連結 UI 控件」這個事實；global reset rules 沒先濾掉 widget anchors。 | base CSS | `_reset.css`: 把 `a:hover { text-decoration: underline }` scope 改成 `a[href]:not([class*="z-"]):hover` — 排除所有 z-* widget anchor，只對純內文超連結加底線 | 2026-05-14 fix complete: live CSS rule set 只剩 `a[href]:not([class*="z-"]):hover` 跟有意的 `.z-a:hover`。menuitem-content 不再 match underline rule。 |
| 2026-05-14 | multislider vertical | 同條 slot 內出現 2 條線（一灰 track 一藍 area），應該重合成一條。Root cause：`.z-multislider-vertical .z-multislider-track { margin-inline: 10px }` 把 track 從 x=314 推到 x=324，但 `.z-sliderbuttons-area` 在 `.z-sliderbuttons { inset: 0 }` 裡的 absolute 定位回到 x=314 — 兩者軸不重合，眼睛看到「兩條 track」。橫向 `margin-block: 10px` 也有對應問題只是視覺上沒那麼刺眼。 | Contract 沒檢查「track 跟 area 必須對齊」這項；rangeslider 跟 multislider 共用 `.z-sliderbuttons-*` 元素但是各自有 track 元素，misalignment 容易被漏掉。 | component CSS | `multislider.css`: 把 `.z-multislider-track` 改為 `position: absolute; inset: 0` — 直接讓 track 跟 area 重合在同一個 inner 容器內。刪掉 horizontal/vertical 特定 track margin。 | 2026-05-14 fix complete: vertical 三個可見元素（track + 2 area）全部 x=314，看不出兩條 track。 |
| 2026-05-14 | rangeslider, multislider（tooltip 一直顯示） | `.z-sliderbuttons-tooltip` 預設可見，使用者沒 hover 也看得到。期望：只在 hover/active/focus thumb 才出現（MD3 / MUI Slider value indicator pattern）。 | Contract 沒檢查 tooltip default visibility；CSS 起手式就把 tooltip 跟 area / button 一起當 always-visible 元素寫。 | component CSS | `rangeslider.css` + `multislider.css`: `.z-sliderbuttons-tooltip { opacity: 0; visibility: hidden; transition: opacity .. , visibility 0s linear var(--zk-motion-duration-short3) }` + `.z-sliderbuttons-button:hover .z-sliderbuttons-tooltip { opacity: 1; visibility: visible; transition-delay: 0s }`。**注意**：tooltip 是 button 的 **descendant**（child），不是 sibling — 首版用 `~` 不對，要用 descendant selector。 | 2026-05-14 fix complete: 預設 opacity=0 visibility=hidden；hover button 才出現。 |
| 2026-05-14 | timebox button | `.z-timebox-input` 跟 `.z-timebox-button` 之間沒有視覺分隔線，難以看出 button 區域。 | Contract 沒檢查 button 是否有 `border-left` 分隔 input 與 button；跟 datebox/combobox（用 split-border）不一致。 | component CSS | `timebox.css`: `.z-timebox-button { border-left: 1px solid var(--zk-color-outline) }` | 2026-05-14 fix complete: borderLeft=1px solid 0.23 已渲染。 |

| 2026-05-14 | rangeslider vertical tooltip | tooltip 距 thumb 太遠（left: -60px），水平方向只用 -36px，左右兩邊不一致。 | Contract 沒對 horizontal/vertical tooltip 跟 thumb 的對稱距離設期望；空間規範不對等。 | component CSS | `rangeslider.css`: vertical `.z-sliderbuttons-tooltip { left: -40px }`（與 horizontal `top: -36px` 同級距） | 2026-05-14 fix complete |
| 2026-05-14 | multislider tooltip | tooltip 跟 thumb 重疊（沒給位置規則）。 | multislider.css 寫 `.z-sliderbuttons-tooltip` 時只設 visual style，沒給 `top/left` 偏移。rangeslider 早就修了同樣問題，沒回頭把 multislider 對齊。 | component CSS | `multislider.css`: 加 horizontal `top: -36px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%)`，vertical `left: -40px; top: 50%; transform: translateY(-50%)` | 2026-05-14 fix complete |
| 2026-05-14 | rating | selected 星星只用 `color: primary` 改變色，icon font 仍是 outline glyph — 視覺上沒「填滿」。 | rating.css 依賴 `z-icon-star` icon font，selected 沒切到 filled glyph。MD3 / MUI Rating 用兩種 icon: outline + filled。 | component CSS | `rating.css`: 用 SVG mask 取代 icon font，outline / filled 切換靠 mask URL（selected 用 fill 完整的 star SVG） | 2026-05-14 fix complete |

| 2026-05-14 | colorbox paletteicon/pickericon | hover effect 沒被注意到 / 看不出 hover | Contract 沒檢查兩個 tab icon 的 hover state-layer。原 CSS `background-color: rgba(0,0,0,0.08)` 太淡，沒走 `--zk-color-on-surface` 8% state-layer。 | component CSS | `colorbox.css`: 改成 `color-mix(in srgb, var(--zk-color-on-surface) 8%, transparent)` + color transition；selected 狀態加 `:hover` 用 on-primary-container 8% 加深 primary-container | 2026-05-14 fix complete (live CSS 兩條 hover rule 都載入) |

| 2026-05-14 | rangeslider drag → 100 (root cause found) | drag thumb 一動就跳 100%。ZK JS (`Rangeslider.ts:350`) `getSliderSize_()` 用 `jq(this.$n_('track')).width()` 讀 track 寬度當分母。我把 `.z-rangeslider-track` 當「填充段」處理，只給 `height: 100%` 沒給 width → track width=0 → drag 公式 `100 * mouseOffset / 0` = Infinity → clamp 到 100。Mold 實際結構：`.z-rangeslider-track` 是**整條 rail**（width:100% height:100%），filled 部分是 `.z-sliderbuttons-area` (由 JS inline style 設 left/width)。 | Skill 沒紀錄「`-track` 是 rail 不是 fill」這個 ZK rangeslider 約定；我從元素名直觀猜錯，contract 也沒檢查 `track.offsetWidth` > 0。 | skill (待寫) + component CSS | `rangeslider.css`: `.z-rangeslider-track { position: absolute; inset: 0; background-color: var(--zk-rangeslider-track-color) }`；移除 inner 上的 track-color background；inner 改為純透明 sizing 容器 | 2026-05-14 fix complete: track horizontal=500×4 vertical=4×500；模擬 drag btn1 從 10% 拖到 30% (預期值)，不再跳 100 |

| 2026-05-14 | colorbox pickericon/paletteicon real click (root cause finally found) | 使用者真實點擊兩個 icon 都沒反應、`.z-colorpicker` 一直 `style="display:none;"`。先前 synthetic `jq.trigger('click')` / `dispatchEvent` 都能切換 — 騙過自己以為「toggle works」。 | DOM 順序：`<paletteicon><pickericon><picker><palette>`，icon 們是先 render 的兄弟元素；picker/palette 後 render，兩邊都 `z-index: auto`，依 painting order picker/palette 蓋住 icon。`elementFromPoint` 在 icon 中心點命中的是 picker (`z-p-8 z-div` from picker-inner)、不是 icon — JS handler bound 在 icon 上，但 hit-testing 把 click 送給 picker，自然 nothing happens。Synthetic trigger 跳過 hit-testing 所以早期測都過。 | Skill: 沒紀錄「ZK colorbox popup 中 paletteicon/pickericon 跟 colorpicker/colorpalette 是 sibling，畫圖順序會讓 icon 被蓋」這個 stacking trap；contract: 沒檢查 `document.elementFromPoint(iconCenter)` 是不是 icon 自己。Synthetic test 永遠看不到 hit-test 問題。 | skill + contract + component CSS | `colorbox.css`: `.z-colorbox-paletteicon, .z-colorbox-pickericon { z-index: 1 }`（含註解說明為何需要）。Skill: 待加 paint-order trap (待寫); Contract: 待加 elementFromPoint check (待寫) | 2026-05-14 fix complete：`elementFromPoint(picCenter)` → `.z-colorbox-pickericon`、`elementFromPoint(palCenter)` → `.z-colorbox-paletteicon`；連續 native click (mousedown+mouseup+click) 雙向切換 OK，`palette.style.display` & `picker.style.display` 跟著反轉。 |

| 2026-05-15 | slider vertical | vertical slider `.z-slider-area` 從**底部**長出來，與 thumb 反向（thumb 在上面、fill 在下面）。期望：area 應該從上面開始長到 thumb 位置。 | Contract 沒檢查「ZK vertical slider 的座標語意」。ZK `Slider._fixPos()` 把 `area.style.height = newPos`、`btn.style.top = newPos`（newPos 是從 inner 頂端往下的 px）。意思是：min=top, max=bottom。area 必須 anchor 在 **top** 才會跟 thumb 同方向；但先前的 CSS 寫 `bottom: 0; top: auto`，造成 fill 跑到反向那端。 | skill + component CSS | `slider.css`: `.z-slider-vertical .z-slider-area { top: 0; bottom: auto }`。Skill: `components/slider.md` 補一段「vertical area 方向／座標語意」。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: 設 curpos=50 後 area top=462 (= center.top)、bottom=512 (= btn.top)，從上面長到 thumb，正確。 |
| 2026-05-15 | searchbox | 外觀完全錯。先前 CSS 把 searchbox 當成單一 text input 寫（input + border + placeholder），實際 DOM 是：label/placeholder trigger + 兩個 icon（clear + caret-down），點擊後 popup 才出現裡頭的 input + checkbox list（multi-select 用 `.z-searchbox-item-check`）。Popup 是經 `makeVParent` 接到 `<body>`。 | Skill 完全沒有 searchbox 元件條目；contract 也沒有 searchbox DOM/use-case 描述；連 `css-file-bundling.md` 都把 searchbox 誤分到 `input.css.dsp`（實際上 searchbox 是 zkmax/inp，自己一個 contract `searchbox.css.dsp`）。整個 harness 沒人知道 searchbox 是什麼樣子。 | skill (new file) + component CSS rewrite + reference fix | `components/searchbox.md` (new): DOM 結構、open/close 行為、`makeVParent` popup、多選 item-check、clear icon 顯隱規則、disabled/focus/open state class、跟 selectbox/combobox/bandbox/chosenbox 的差異。`searchbox.css`: 完整改寫，trigger 樣式（label + clear icon + caret-down icon）、popup 樣式（search input + 滾動 cave + item + item-check + selected/active state）；popup default `display: none`，`.z-searchbox-open .z-searchbox-popup { display: flex }`（mold 沒在 popup 上寫 inline display style，沒這條 popup 開機時會直接外露）。`reference/css-file-bundling.md`: 從 `input.css.dsp` 移除 searchbox。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: trigger 是 40-tall chip 樣式（placeholder/label + clear×/caret▼）；點擊後 popup 出現（search input + 7 items with globe icon）；選 "North America" 後 trigger 顯示 truncated label + clear icon 出現；disabled 變淡。 |
| 2026-05-15 | searchbox popup default visibility | 開發 verify 時發現：mold 沒給 popup inline `style="display:none"`，所以只要 CSS 寫 `.z-searchbox-popup { display: flex }`，popup 一 render 就會跑出來掛在每個 searchbox 旁邊。 | 過去 harness 沒留意到「ZK 多數 popup 是 JS 用 inline style 控制顯隱、但 searchbox 的 popup 在 render 階段就已經存在於 trigger 內部、JS 也沒幫忙加 hidden style」。多數 ZK floating popup 走 `display: none` initial（透過 mold inline 或 widget JS），但 searchbox 走另一條路。 | component CSS（記在 skill 的 floating-popup-in-body 上比較對） | `searchbox.css`: popup default `display: none`，靠 `.z-searchbox-open .z-searchbox-popup { display: flex }` 開時切回；ZK 的 `slideDown()` 會 inline 設 `display: block` 蓋過。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: 初始畫面只看到 2 個 trigger chip，無 popup leak；點 trigger 才出現 popup。 |

| 2026-05-15 | grid header (and listbox/tree headers) | 表頭使用 `surface-container-low` 底色，與 Mira Simple Table / MD3 Data Table spec 不符（兩者都是無底色，只靠 font-weight 500 + border-bottom 區隔）。 | Skill 沒寫「ZK data-component 表頭應該怎麼跟 reference theme 對齊」這條規則；過去三個檔（grid.css / listbox.css / tree.css）各自寫了 tinted header，沒有 cross-component 一致性檢查。 | component CSS (3 files) + design doc | grid.css `.z-column`、listbox.css `.z-listheader` / `.z-listhead`、tree.css `.z-treecol` / `.z-treecols` 移除 `background-color`（讓 surface 透出來）；保留 border-bottom 跟 font-weight 作為視覺分隔。`doc/spec/DESIGN.md` 補一段 data-table header 設計決定（對齊 MUI Simple Table + MD3 Data Table）。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: grid / listbox / tree 三張 preview page 表頭全部 unfilled、字色 on-surface-variant、border-bottom outline-variant 區隔 body。視覺三者一致。 |

| 2026-05-15 | grid footer (`.z-grid-footer`, `.z-footer`) | footer 字距 cell 邊緣太緊（padding 只有 spacing-2/4），跟 header 與 body cell 不一致；MD3 spec 規定 data-table footer 應跟 body 同 padding。發現實際 DOM 是 `TR.z-foot > TD.z-footer > DIV.z-footer-content`，不是 `.z-cell` 或 `.z-row-content`。 | contract 沒檢查「footer cell padding 是否與 body 一致」這項；先前 css 把 selector 寫成 `.z-foot .z-cell / .z-row-content` 完全沒命中真實 DOM。 | component CSS | `grid.css`: `.z-foot .z-footer { padding: 0 }`、`.z-foot .z-footer-content { padding: var(--zk-grid-cell-padding) }`（content padding 16px = 跟 body 同 spacing-4）。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: `.z-footer` padding=0、`.z-footer-content` padding=16px，跟 body cell 一致。 |
| 2026-05-15 | grid auxhead (`.z-auxhead` row + `.z-auxheader` cells) | multi-level header 分組 row，先前用 `surface-container` 整片填色，跟 column-header 透明處理衝突。User decision: row 用 surface-container-low **faint tint**，cells 透明，靠 row-band 顯示「parent group」階層。 | Skill 沒寫「auxhead 是 ZK-only multi-level header；視覺層級該怎麼跟一般 column-header 區隔」的規則。Contract 沒檢查既有 `mesh/css/auxhead.css` 跟 `grid.css` 的 auxhead 規則重複（且 mesh 版有 `!important` 會 win）。 | component CSS (mesh shared) + design doc + skill | `mesh/css/auxhead.css`（shared）改寫: `.z-auxhead { background-color: surface-container-low }`、`.z-auxheader { background-color: transparent }`；移除 `!important`；移除 `grid.css` 中的 duplicate。`doc/spec/DESIGN.md`: 補 auxhead band 設計決定。Skill: `components/data-components.md` 補 auxhead section。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: `.z-auxhead` bg = surface-container-low (rgb 247,249,252)、`.z-auxheader` bg transparent；視覺層級從 row-band 表達。 |
| 2026-05-15 | grid frozen columns | `frozen columns="2"` 鎖前 N 欄、後面 column 用 `.z-frozen-inner` 上的 horizontal scrollbar 滑動。ZK 不是 CSS `position: sticky`，是 JS 把 columns past N 用 translateX 平移；DOM 結構是 `.z-frozen > .z-frozen-body + .z-frozen-inner(overflow-x:scroll) + .z-frozen-right + .z-clear`，frozen rows/cells/columns 上掛 `.z-frozen-col` / `.z-grid-frozen`。發現舊版 `mesh/css/frozen.css` 把 `.z-frozen-inner` 當 sticky-pane wrapper 寫（用 `position:absolute + overflow:hidden`），跟實際 ZK JS 機制衝突，讓 scrollbar 出不來。 | Skill 沒紀錄「ZK frozen = JS-driven translate；DOM 結構與所有 `.z-frozen-*` class」。Contract 沒檢查 stale `frozen.css` 跟 ZK 實際 mold 結構是否吻合。 | component CSS (mesh shared) + skill | `mesh/css/frozen.css`（shared）改寫: `.z-frozen-inner { overflow-x: scroll }`（讓 native scrollbar 出來，ZK JS inline-styles 寬高）、scrollbar 顏色用 outline-variant token、`.z-frozen-col:not(:has(+ .z-frozen-col))` 在 last frozen cell 加 border-inline-end + box-shadow elevation 表達 pane boundary。移除 `grid.css` duplicate。Skill: `components/data-components.md` 補 frozen-columns section（含 DOM 結構、JS-driven 提示、`:has()` boundary selector）。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: `.z-frozen-inner` overflow-x=scroll；最後一個 frozen-col 有 1px outline-variant border + 2px×4px box-shadow 邊界；scrollbar 顏色走 token。boundary selector 用 `:not(:has(+ .z-frozen-col))` 正確命中末欄。 |

| 2026-05-15 | grid frozen visual indicators (refinement of前一條) | (1) `.z-frozen-body`（位於 frozen 欄下方的 spacer）視覺上看不出來「上面那兩欄是 frozen」。(2) `.z-frozen-inner` 的 horizontal scrollbar 在 macOS overlay-scrollbar 環境下 ZK JS 量到 6px → tray 跟 inner 都被 inline-style 成 height:6px、太薄看不到 scrollbar。比較 ZK 原版（SUPPORT project）量到 15px 是正常的，差異來自 jq.scrollbarWidth() return value。 | Skill 沒寫「frozen-body 是用來指示 frozen pane 範圍的 spacer」、也沒紀錄「ZK 用 native scrollbar 量測來設定 tray height、不同 OS / 瀏覽器設定會有差」。Contract 沒檢查 tray height 是否大到可見、frozen-body 是否有 pane 標示。 | component CSS (mesh shared) | `mesh/css/frozen.css`: `.z-frozen { min-height: 12px }` (覆寫 ZK inline 6px，min-height 一定贏 inline height)、`.z-frozen-body { background: surface-container-low + 1px outline-variant border-inline-end + 2px×4px box-shadow elevation + min-height: 12px }`（讓使用者看到 pane 結束邊）、`.z-frozen-inner { overflow-x: scroll; min-height: 12px }`（讓 styled WebKit scrollbar 有空間 render）。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: tray h=12px, body h=12px + faint band bg + boundary shadow, inner h=12px + scroll, scrollWidth (1600) > clientWidth (1434) 可滑動。 |
| 2026-05-15 | listbox click → page jumps to top (CRITICAL bug) | 點選任何 listitem 後整頁被 scroll 到 `scrollY=0`。Root cause：`.z-listbox-body` 是 `position: static`，所以裡面的 `.z-focus-a` 雖然是 `position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0;`，會去找 nearest positioned ancestor — 找不到、就 fallback 到 `<body>`，等於把 trap div 放在 viewport (0,0)。當 ZK `SelectWidget._focusAnchor` 呼叫 `focusEl.focus()` 時，瀏覽器為了讓 focused element 可見會 scroll page to (0,0)，所以整頁跳上去。同樣機制 grid、tree 都會中。 | Skill 沒紀錄「`.z-focus-a` 是 select widget 的 focus-trap，依賴 nearest positioned ancestor」。Contract 沒驗證 `.z-focus-a.offsetParent` 是不是 listbox-body / tree-body / grid-body。calendar.css 跟 menu.css 早就有 `.z-focus-a` style，但 sel/css/listbox.css 沒有 — 一個跨組件規則沒人 enforce。 | component CSS (3 files) + skill | `listbox.css`、`tree.css`、`grid.css` 三檔分別加 `.z-listbox-body { position: relative }` / `.z-tree-body { position: relative }` / `.z-grid-body { position: relative }` 與 scoped `.z-focus-a { position: absolute; w/h: 0; opacity: 0; pointer-events: none }`，確保 trap div 錨點正確不會把焦點推到 viewport (0,0)。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: `body_position: relative`、`focusA.offsetParent: DIV.z-listbox-body`（之前是 BODY）。模擬「先 scroll 到 y=800、點 listitem」場景：afterY=800, pageJumped=false (之前 delta=-800)。 |
| 2026-05-15 | listheader-button (`.z-listheader-button`) | listbox 的 column-action button（menupopup="auto" 觸發），跟 grid `.z-column-button` 是 sibling concept，但 listbox.css 完全沒寫 button 樣式跟 hover-reveal rule。 | Skill 沒寫「listheader 跟 column header 一樣有 menupopup-button、行為一致」這條 cross-widget rule。Contract 沒檢查 listheader-button visibility / position。 | component CSS + skill | `listbox.css`: 複製 grid column-button 的 progressive-disclosure pattern（opacity 0→1 on `:hover`/`.z-listheader-hover`/`:focus-visible`，icon-button 24×24 fully-rounded state-layer）。Skill (`components/data-components.md`): 把 column-button section 擴寫成 "Header action button"，涵蓋 grid `.z-column-button` + listbox `.z-listheader-button` + tree `.z-treecol-button` 的共同 pattern。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: `.z-listheader-button` default opacity 0 (with transition:none 驗證)、加 `.z-listheader-hover` 後 opacity=1、width 24px、fully rounded。 |

| 2026-05-15 | grid frozen layout (refinement) | `.z-frozen-body` 跟 `.z-frozen-inner` 沒並排顯示，inner 跑到 body 下面（垂直 stack）。ZK mold 規定它們要側邊排列（body float:left, inner 緊接在右側），scrollbar 才會出現在 inner 上、視覺上跟 frozen pane 連續。 | 我們的前一條 frozen 修法 (`mesh/css/frozen.css`) 只搬了 ZK CSS 的部分屬性、漏掉 `.z-frozen-body { float: left }` 跟 `.z-frozen-right { float: right }` 跟 `.z-clear { clear: both }`。Contract 沒檢查 layout 是否 side-by-side。 | component CSS (mesh shared) | `mesh/css/frozen.css`: `.z-frozen { overflow: hidden }`（補回 ZK 原本的 clip）、`.z-frozen-body { float: left; overflow: hidden }`、`.z-frozen-right { float: right; overflow: hidden }`、`.z-frozen-inner > div { height: 100% }`。並排靠 float 達成、`.z-clear` 收尾。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: body float=left, body.right(432) === inner.left(432)、sideBySide=true sameRow=true、inner.scrollWidth(1600) > clientWidth(1434) 可橫向 scroll。 |
| 2026-05-15 | listbox-grouping `.z-listgroup` / `.z-listgroupfoot` 雙重邊框 | `.z-listgroup` (group header row) 跟 `.z-listgroupfoot` (group subtotal row) 在 td、`.z-listcell-cnt`、`.z-listcell-content` 三層都套 `border-top + border-bottom`，渲染出三條疊在一起的線（上 3 條 + 下 3 條），視覺看起來 odd。 | Contract 沒檢查「邊框落在哪一個層級」、所以把同樣的 border 重複放到三層元素上沒被警告。前版 css 設計者把 cell-level border 當 row-level border 寫。 | component CSS | `listbox.css`: 把 border 從 `td/cnt/content` 三層全收起來，改放單一條在 row 上 — `.z-listgroup { background: surface-container; border-bottom: 1px outline-variant }`、`.z-listgroupfoot { background: surface-container-low; border-top: 1px outline-variant }`。Cell 級用 `background: transparent` 讓 row band 透出。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: groupTd_borderTop/Bottom=0px none、groupContent_borderTop/Bottom=0px none、`.z-listgroup` row 只有 1px bottom divider、`.z-listgroupfoot` row 只有 1px top divider。 |
| 2026-05-15 | paging os mold first/prev/next/last 圖示錯誤 | OS mold 渲染 4 個 nav 按鈕（First/Prev/Next/Last），前版 CSS 只用 `:first-child` (給 First+Prev 同一個 chevron-left 圖示) 跟 `:last-child` (給 Next+Last 同一個 chevron-right 圖示) — 結果 First 不是 double-left、Last 不是 double-right。 | Skill 沒紀錄「os mold 4 個 nav 按鈕怎麼用 CSS selector 區分」、contract 也沒驗證圖示對應方向。 | component CSS | `paging.css`: 用 `:nth-child(N)` / `:nth-last-child(2)` 區分 4 個位置：`:first-child`→First (chevrons-left, 2 paths)、`:nth-child(2)`→Prev (chevron-left)、`:nth-last-child(2)`→Next (chevron-right)、`:last-child`→Last (chevrons-right, 2 paths)。Selector 依賴「page-number li 不會出現在 1、2、last-1、last 這 4 個位置」這個事實。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: 4 個 nav button mask SVG path 內容跟預期 chevron direction 一致（First=m11/m18, Prev=m15 18, Next=m9 18, Last=m6/m13）。 |
| 2026-05-15 | selectbox popup 採新的 Customizable Select 規格 | HTML spec (Chrome 130+) 允許 `select { appearance: base-select }` 後用 CSS 直接 style popup (`::picker(select)`)、option (`option:hover/checked`)、checkmark (`option::checkmark`)、arrow (`::picker-icon`)、selected indicator (`selectedcontent`)。前版 CSS 只 style 觸發框，popup 還是 native browser 樣式。 | Skill 沒紀錄 customizable select 規格、contract 沒紀錄該如何用 `@supports (appearance: base-select)` opt-in 又保持舊瀏覽器的 fallback。 | component CSS + skill | `selectbox.css`: 新增 `@supports (appearance: base-select) { ... }` 區塊，在內：opt-in `appearance: base-select`；popup `::picker(select)` 用 MD3 elevation-3 + surface-container + shape-medium；option flex layout + state-layer hover/active/checked（8%/12% on-surface 跟 secondary-container 對選中）；`option::checkmark` 用 SVG mask 渲染；`::picker-icon` 用 on-surface-variant、open 時 rotate 180deg；`selectedcontent` styling。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: CSS.supports('appearance', 'base-select')=true、sel_appearance=base-select、option display=flex padding=8px 12px borderRadius=8px cursor=pointer 全部 cascade。Popup 顯示需 user gesture，UI 上點開即驗證。 |

| 2026-05-22 | panel | no-title panel (panels 4–6 in "Without Toolbar" section) 頂端出現一條可見的 minus-icon 橫條 (`.z-panel-drag-button`)。ZK mold 在無 `title=` 且無 `<caption>` 子元件時，渲染 `.z-panel-drag-button` 取代 `.z-panel-head`；該元素是純 JS drag-wire anchor，DK 主題用 `display: none` 隱藏它。Marble theme 的 panel.css 沒有這條規則，導致橫條可見。ZK 在相同條件下也會對 root 加上 `.z-panel-noheader` class（來自 `Panel.domClass_()` in `Panel.ts`）。 | Skill `components/panel.md` DOM 結構錯誤（缺 `.z-panel-drag-button` 分支）且沒有記錄「永遠 `display: none`」的要求；contract `panel.md` 沒有 no-title panel 的 c8 check；CSS 起手式沒參照 DK 基準。 | skill + contract | Skill: `components/panel.md` 補正完整 DOM 結構、新增 `z-panel-drag-button` 段落說明 render 條件與 `display: none` 要求、補 `z-panel-noheader` state class；Contract: `panel.md` 新增 c8（`display: none`）及 no-title states-to-evaluate 項目；CSS: `panel.css` 把 drag-button 規則改為 `display: none`（移除先前的 `cursor/padding/opacity`）。 | 補完 skill + contract + css 三層後，panel 的 no-title 情形完整記錄；drag-button 在 3 個 no-title panel 的 computed display 均為 none，offsetWidth/Height = 0。 |

| 2026-05-15 | listbox-grouping (`.z-listgroup-inner`) | group header row 的 td `colspan="5"` 沒有跨欄、只占第一欄寬度（1318/1838px）— 視覺上 group title 跟一般 cell 一樣窄，破壞 grouping 視覺結構。Root cause：先前 CSS 在 `td.z-listgroup-inner` 設 `display: flex` (為了用 flexbox 排 icon + text)，但 `display: flex` 會關掉 `display: table-cell`，HTML table layout 直接 ignore 該 td 的 `colspan` 屬性 — cell 退回單一 column 寬。 | Skill 沒紀錄「給 `<td>` 設 `display: flex/grid` 會 break colspan」這條 HTML table 規則；contract 也沒檢查 group row td.width === row.width。對 grid `.z-grid-foot` colspan 也可能有同樣陷阱。 | skill + component CSS | `listbox.css`: `td.z-listgroup-inner { display: table-cell }` 還回 table cell 行為；flex 排版搬到 `.z-listgroup-inner > .z-listcell-content { display: flex; align-items: center; gap }`。Skill (`components/data-components.md`): 新增 "colspan + display:flex" trap section。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: td_display=table-cell, td.width(1837) === row.width(1837), spansAll=true。 |
| 2026-05-15 | listbox-header / listheader 長文字 + hover-button 重疊 | menupopup 或 sortable 的 `.z-listheader` 內部會藏一個 hover-revealed `.z-listheader-button` (`opacity: 0` → 1 on hover)。先前 header rule 用 `white-space: nowrap` + `overflow: hidden` + `ellipsis`，當文字長時被切掉；hover 出現 button 又跟文字重疊在右邊區域。 | Skill 沒寫「listheader 含 button 時要 reserve padding-right 給 button slot；header 文字應允許 wrap」這條設計規則。Contract 沒檢查文字行為 (nowrap vs wrap) 跟 button overlap area。 | component CSS + skill | `listbox.css`: 改 `.z-listheader { white-space: normal; overflow-wrap: anywhere }` 允許長文字 wrap；新增 `.z-listheader:has(.z-listheader-button) { padding-right: calc(spacing-1 + 24px + spacing-2) }`（=36px）reserve button slot — 注意 ZK 把 button 放在 `.z-listheader-content` **裡**，`:has()` 用 descendant 不能用 `>` 直接子代。Skill 補「header text wrap + button slot reservation」說明。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: whiteSpace=normal, paddingRight=36px 在全部 5 個 header 上, 短文字行還是單行不撐高、長文字 wrap 2 行；button hover 時不再蓋文字。 |
| 2026-05-15 | listbox-header / checkbox-only column 圖示右邊被切 | `<listheader checkable="true" width="40px">` 用 `.z-listheader-checkable` span 包 16×16 check icon。先前 header rule padding 16px 四邊 → 40 - 16 - 16 = 8px 可用區，16px icon 超出右邊被 `overflow: hidden` 切掉。 | Contract 沒檢查「窄 checkbox column 的 padding 是否塞得下 icon」。先前的 width 規則只設了 `.z-listheader-checkable` (span 自己) 沒處理 outer `.z-listheader` cell padding。 | component CSS | `listbox.css`: `.z-listheader:has(.z-listheader-checkable) { padding-left: spacing-1; padding-right: spacing-1 }`（4px 兩側）讓 40px cell 留 32px content 區 — 16px icon 完整顯示，跟 listcell 同步。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: checkbox-cell padding 16px 4px, headerW=40, iconW=16, iconLeft=881, headerRight=905 → 右邊還有 8px 餘裕，icon 完整不再被切。 |
| 2026-05-15 | selectbox `::picker(select):not(:open)` 永遠 match → popup 永遠隱形 | Customizable Select spec：`:open` pseudo-class 屬於 `<select>` element，**不**屬於 `::picker` pseudo-element。先前的 CSS 用 `.z-selectbox::picker(select):not(:open) { opacity: 0 }` — 但 picker 本身不會有 `:open` 狀態，`:not(:open)` 永遠成立，所以 opacity 永遠 0、popup 開了也看不見。 | Skill (selectbox.md Customizable Select 段) 沒紀錄 `:open` 套用對象是 select（originating element）不是 picker。新規格陷阱沒人示警。 | component CSS + skill | `selectbox.css`: 刪掉 broken `.z-selectbox::picker(select):not(:open)` rule；改用 `.z-selectbox:open::picker(select) { opacity: 1; transform: 0 }` 把開啟態加在 select 側 (`:open` chained on select, then `::picker(select)`)。base `::picker(select)` 設 opacity 0 + translateY(-4) 為起始/關閉態，加 `transition-behavior: allow-discrete` + `@starting-style` block 讓 top-layer fade-in/out 動畫順暢。Skill (`components/selectbox.md`): 補一段「`:open` lives on the select, not the picker」trap 與正確 selector pattern。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: rules dump 顯示新規則 `.z-selectbox:open::picker` + `@starting-style { .z-selectbox:open::p }` 已 parse；舊的永真 `:not(:open)` 完全移除。popup 可用 user click 開啟驗證視覺（CSS 結構正確）。 |
| 2026-05-15 | menubar 全部 menuitem 文字看不見 | `.z-menubar { background-color: transparent; color: on-primary }` + `.z-menu-content / .z-menuitem-content { color: on-primary }` — `on-primary` 是白色，但 root 沒有 primary 底色 (transparent)，外層 page 又是 surface (`rgb(247,249,252)`)，白字落在白底上完全看不見。先前的設計顯然假設外層會包 primary container，但 preview / 多數實際 use case 都沒包。 | Skill 沒紀錄「ZK menubar 預設 surface tone 應該是什麼」這條 MD3 設計決定；contract 沒做 menubar contrast (text vs bg) 檢查。先前撰寫者把 menubar 當 top-app-bar primary variant 寫，但 spec 預設應該是 surface variant。 | component CSS | `menu.css`: `.z-menubar { background-color: surface-container; color: on-surface }`，`.z-menu-content` / `.z-menubar .z-menuitem-content` 兩處 `color: on-primary → on-surface`，state-layer overlay `background-color: on-primary → on-surface`。MD3 navigation/top-app-bar surface variant pattern。 | 2026-05-15 fix complete: bar_bg=rgb(240,244,250) (surface-container), bar_color/content_color=rgba(0,0,0,0.87) (on-surface)，對比強烈、所有 menuitem label 清晰可讀。 |

right: 0; top: 0`；(3) Contract c22c/c22d 用 `padding` 而非 `margin`，padding 在 content-box 模型下會與 ZK 的 inline `width` 加乘 → 壓縮可視 strip；(4) Evaluator §3c layout sweep 不檢查 toolbar 在 tabbox 內是否 absolute，也不檢查 `.z-tabbox-scroll > .z-tabs` 的 padding 為 0；spec 全綠仍 layout 壞掉。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill (`.claude/skills/zk-component-rules/components/tabbox.md` `## Layout invariants`): 新增三條 invariant — (a) `.z-toolbar.z-toolbar-tabs` 必為 `position: absolute`、(b) `.z-tabs` 在 scroll mode 必須用 `margin` 不可用 `padding`、(c) flex-column root 允許但前提是 scroll-buttons + toolbar 全部 absolute；Skill (`toolbar.md`) 新增 `## Context class: .z-toolbar-tabs` 章節說明 ZK 在 Tabbox parent 下自動補 context class；Contract (`doc/contracts/tabbox.md`) c22c/c22d 把 `padding` 改成 `margin`、新增 c22e (toolbar absolute)、c22f (root overflow:hidden)；Agent (`.claude/agents/zk-theme-evaluator.md` §3c): 新增第 5 點「Toolbar-in-container check」量測 `.z-toolbar-tabs` 必須 absolute、第 6 點「In-flow-overlay-margin check」確認 `.z-tabbox-scroll > .z-tabs` `padding=0` 且 `margin` 為預期值；Component CSS (`tabbox.css`): 把 `padding: 0 40px` 改 `margin: 0 40px`、新增 `.z-tabbox > .z-toolbar.z-toolbar-tabs { position: absolute; | 2026-05-21 | tabbox vertical (Vertical Left) | `.z-tabbox-up-scroll` 與 `.z-tabbox-down-scroll` 寬度 80px，而 `.z-tabs` / `.z-tab` 都是 120px，視覺上 scroll 圖示比 tab 短一截。原因：使用者在 ZUL 寫 `<tabs width="80px">`，ZK 把 80 設成 `.z-tabs.style.width`；我們的 CSS `.z-tabbox-vertical .z-tabs { min-width: 120px }` 把 tabs 撐到 120px，但 `Tabs._scrollcheck` (line 425) `u.style.width = d.style.width = tabs.style.width` 把 80 propagate 給 scroll buttons，這條 inline 80 沒有 CSS min-width 兜底所以按鈕真的渲染成 80。 | (1) Skill 沒提到 ZK 把 `.z-tabs.style.width` 複製給 up/down scroll button 的事實；(2) Contract 沒檢測「相依 inline width」這條 cross-element 規則；(3) Evaluator §3c 4 條 layout-regression 不會比對「兩個元素的 inline width 應該一致或都被 CSS 兜底」。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill (`tabbox.md` Layout invariants): 新增「Vertical-orient scroll buttons inherit `.z-tabs` inline width」條款，要求 vertical scroll buttons 必須複製相同 min-width；Contract (`doc/contracts/tabbox.md`): 新增 c22g `.z-tabbox-scroll > .z-tabbox-{up,down}-scroll { min-width: 120px }`；Evaluator §3c: 新增 check 7「Inherited-inline-width check」量測 ZK runtime 已知會跨元素複製 inline width 的情境；Component CSS (`tabbox.css`): vertical scroll buttons block 加 `min-width: 120px`。 | 2026-05-21 fix complete: vertical scroll buttons offsetWidth=120 等於 .z-tabs offsetWidth、等於 .z-tab offsetWidth。 |

| 2026-05-21 | tabbox accordion (Accordion Mold) | `height="300px" mold="accordion"` 五個 section 的情形下，被選 tab 的 `.z-tabpanel-content` 高度幾乎為 0（只剩 padding 32px），內容文字完全不可見。同樣的 ZUL 在 usecase2 (沒設 height) 是正常。原因鏈：(a) base `.z-tabpanel { min-height: 80px }` 未在 accordion override，5 個 section 變成 5×80=400px 已超過 tabbox 300px；(b) 即使 reset 了 min-height，base `.z-tab-content { padding: 12px 16px }` 與 accordion `.z-tab` padding 12px 疊加，header 變 68px、5×68=340 仍超過 300；(c) `Tabpanel._fixPanelHgh` (Tabpanel.ts line 166-171) 用 `tbx.offsetHeight - Σ(sibling offsetHeight) - tab.offsetHeight` 算 cave 高度，算出負數就 `jq.px0` clamp 到 0，把 inline `style.height: 0px` 寫進去。 | (1) Skill 完全沒提 `Tabpanel._fixPanelHgh` 這條算式以及它對 accordion section 高度的隱含 budget；(2) Skill 也沒警告 base `.z-tabpanel { min-height }` 與 base `.z-tab-content { padding }` 會「貫穿」accordion override 造成 budget 爆掉；(3) Contract 沒檢測 accordion section 與 header 的高度上限；(4) Evaluator §3c layout sweep 不檢查「inline style.height === 0px 但 scrollHeight > 0」這種 animation-clamped 狀態，spec 全綠仍會看不到內容。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill (`tabbox.md`): 新增「Accordion-mold section min-height MUST be reset to 0」與「Accordion `.z-tab-content` MUST reset its base padding to 0」兩條 invariant，並把 `_fixPanelHgh` 算式寫進來；Contract (`doc/contracts/tabbox.md`): 新增 c22h `.z-tabbox-accordion .z-tabpanel { min-height: 0 }`、c22i `.z-tabbox-accordion .z-tab-content { padding: 0 }`；Evaluator §3c: 新增 check 8「Animation-stuck-on-zero check」量測 `cave.style.height === '0px' && cave.scrollHeight > 0`；Component CSS (`tabbox.css`): accordion `.z-tabpanel` 加 `min-height: 0`、accordion `.z-tab-content` 加 `padding: 0`、accordion `.z-tab` padding 從 12/16 改 8/16 + min-height 從 52 改 48（aligned to MD3 48px 標準）。 | 2026-05-21 fix complete: tabbox 標準頁 height=300 + 5 sections，每個 header 48px、總高 48+4×49=244、剩餘 56px 給選中 cave、內容文字可見。 |


| 2026-05-21 | tabbox (Vertical Right — orient=right 回歸) | `usecase/index.zul#tabbox` 的 Vertical Right 區段 `z-tabbox-up-scroll` / `z-tabbox-down-scroll` 顯示在 tabbox 的左邊，而 tabs 在右邊；scroll icon 沒跟 tabs 同側、視覺斷裂。User 報告「這個問題我之前應該回報過」表示先前修過後又被改壞，要求「想一個方法避免再次造成錯誤」。Root cause：CSS 把 4 個 directional scroll button 寫成 4 條 single-line rule，其中 `.z-tabbox-up-scroll { left: 0 }`、`.z-tabbox-down-scroll { left: 0 }` 是 horizontal 邊界共用 default，對 `.z-tabbox-left`/`.z-tabbox-vertical` 正確（tabs 在左），但 `.z-tabbox-right` 沒有 override 還是繼承 `left: 0`，按鈕落到左邊。 | (1) Skill 沒有把「vertical scroll button 必須與 tabs 同側」寫成 layout invariant — 上次修完後跟著前後 round 一起 refactor，這條約束沒寫進來、後續 reformat 時不知不覺改回對稱的 `left: 0`；(2) Contract 沒有特別 c22 row 規範 `.z-tabbox-right` 的 scroll-button 位置；(3) Evaluator §3c 8 條 layout-regression 不檢查「scroll button 是否與對應的 tabs 在同一側」這條方向性 invariant、spec 全綠仍然錯位。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill (`tabbox.md` Layout invariants): 新增「Vertical scroll-button horizontal anchor MUST follow the tabs' side」條款，明寫 `.z-tabbox-left`/`.z-tabbox-vertical` 用 `left: 0`、`.z-tabbox-right` 需 explicit override `left: auto; right: 0`，並警告「regression-prone — 重構共用 directional rules 時容易遺失」；Contract (`doc/contracts/tabbox.md`): 新增 c22j `.z-tabbox-right > .z-tabbox-{up,down}-scroll { left: auto; right: 0 }`；Evaluator §3c: 新增 check 9「Scroll-button-side-mirror check」量測 vertical orient 中 scroll button `offsetLeft` 是否落在 tabs 同側（left/right half）；Component CSS (`tabbox.css`): 在 4 條 directional rule 後緊接著加 `.z-tabbox-right > .z-tabbox-up-scroll, .z-tabbox-right > .z-tabbox-down-scroll { left: auto; right: 0 }` 並寫 comment 警示這條 override 不能被「合併簡化」掉。 | 2026-05-21 fix complete (待驗證): CSS override 已加；skill / contract / evaluator 三層都記錄了 invariant + automated check，下次有人 refactor 共用 directional rule 時，evaluator check 9 會立刻 FAIL。 |

| 2026-05-21 | tabbox accordion (Accordion Mold border-radius mismatch) | `.z-tabbox-accordion` 設 `border-radius: var(--zk-shape-corner-medium)`，但其下的 children（headers `.z-tab`、`.z-tabpanel-content` cave）都是方角；rounded parent + 方角 children 在四個角落露出 children 的 sharp 邊。一般 fix 是 `overflow: hidden` 把 children 剪到圓角範圍內，但 accordion 必須避用 `overflow: hidden`（會干擾 `jq.slideDown` 對 cave 的 height 量測）。User 引用 MD style 表示「children 也不應該有 border-radius」、要求把 parent 拿掉 border-radius 走 flat 設計。 | (1) Skill / contract 沒有寫「accordion mold MUST NOT use border-radius」這條結構性禁止；(2) Skill 沒提「rounded parent + square children + 無 overflow 剪裁 = corners 漏出」這條跨組件 CSS 陷阱；(3) Evaluator §3c 8 條 layout-regression 沒檢查「parent 有 border-radius 但 overflow ≠ hidden 且 children 方角」這個 visual-defect pattern。 | skill + contract + agent | Skill (`tabbox.md`): 新增「Accordion mold MUST NOT set `border-radius` on `.z-tabbox-accordion`」invariant，附帶解釋為何 `overflow: hidden` 不能用；Contract (`doc/contracts/tabbox.md`): c10 由 `--zk-shape-corner-medium` 改為 `0`、新增 c22k 描述禁止 border-radius；Evaluator §3c: 新增 check 10「Parent-radius-without-clip check」量測 `border-radius > 0` 且 `overflow ≠ hidden` 且 first/last children 在對應角為方角時 FAIL；Component CSS (`tabbox.css`): 移除 `.z-tabbox-accordion` 的 `border-radius` 並加 inline comment 說明禁止改動。 | 2026-05-21 fix complete (待驗證): CSS 已移除 border-radius；skill / contract / evaluator 三層都記錄。新的 check 10 是通用規則，未來任何 component 若把 rounded parent 配方角 children 但無剪裁，自動會被 evaluator 標出。 |

Absolute children cannot escape an ancestor's `overflow:hidden`, so the row clipped the overhang. Live re-verified: `html` height 918, `body.clientHeight` 918; colorbox below-click closes (target `BODY`, float-up fires); sibling sweep — combobox dropdown below-click also now closes (target `BODY`, `closed:true`). `html { height: 100% }` confirmed scrollbar-aware: forced horizontal overflow → `body.clientHeight` shrinks 918→912, NO spurious vertical scrollbar — so the original 2026-06-12 scrollbar bug stays fixed too. |

| 2026-06-15 | datebox, timebox (follow-up to wheel mold; same day) | Follow-up to the wheel-mold fix above. User re-reported a subtler mobile bug: tapping the datebox **input** opens the wheel sheet ~18px **too low** (it overshoots below the viewport bottom, clipping the OK/Cancel row), while tapping the **icon** lands the sheet flush. Root cause is a ZK framework quirk, not the wheel CSS: `CalendarPop._syncPosition` (datebox-touch.ts) anchors the bottom sheet with `top = (zk.ios?window.innerHeight:jq.innerHeight()) + window.scrollY` then calls `$pp.zk.makeVParent()` to detach the popup to `<body>` — and makeVParent (via the iOS `_fixedVParent` override) **inflates the inline `top` by ~18px** (an offset-parent delta). That inflation is only undone if a *second* `_syncPosition` fires (from `onSize`). The **icon** tap happens to trigger that second sync (`top 1130→1112`); the **input** tap does not (focusing the readonly `<input>` suppresses the extra resize/onSize), so it stays at the inflated `top:1130` → 18px overshoot. Measured live (iPad UA, viewport 1112): input `top:1130 overshoot:+18`, icon `top:1112 overshoot:0`. The sheet was also left-aligned at `x:0` (datebox-touch never sets `left`, unlike combo-touch which centres). | The 2026-06-15 wheel test taps the `.z-datebox-button` (icon) — the one target that *happens* to self-correct via the second sync — so it never exercised the input tap that stays mis-anchored. | 2026-06-16 | window, panel, groupbox (+ theme-wide, root cause) | User-reported: `.z-window-header` (and panel/groupbox headers) rendered **much taller** than the design — window 88px, panel 80px, groupbox 73px. Root cause is **NOT a token/value error** (the 16px header padding already matches MUI DialogTitle/CardHeader): the packaged (minified) build was **missing the universal `*{box-sizing:border-box}` reset**. CleanCSS 5.3.3 (`scripts/build-css.js`, level 1) has a bug — a bare layer-order statement `@layer a, b;` is dropped **together with the single CSS rule that immediately follows it**, and in `_reset.css` that following rule is the box-sizing reset. With the reset gone, every element relying on it computed as `content-box`, so header `min-height` became **additive** with padding (window 56+32=88, panel 48+32=80, groupbox 48+24=73) instead of inclusive. A second, related cause for narrow no-border panels: the title-mold renders the title as a **bare text node** (not a `.z-caption` child), so the existing `.z-caption-content/.z-label` nowrap rules never reached it → long titles wrapped to 2 lines and inflated the header. | Two blind spots. (1) The **dev** build (unminified) keeps the reset, so the bug only existed in *packaged* CSS — the live preview the screenshot baselines were captured against had border-box, so the visual baselines were correct while production silently drifted. No test or contract asserted computed header *height* (window.md c4 asserted header *padding* = 16px, which was correct), so a content-box blow-up to 88px passed every check. (2) The harness never minified-and-measured: it measured the dev CSS. (3) Bare-title-node wrap: the harness `window`/`panel` gallery baselines capture wide demos whose short titles don't wrap; the narrow (280px) no-border panel that wraps was never measured for height. | impl (build) + contract | Build: `scripts/build-css.js` `minifyCss` — extract bare `@layer …;` statements (strict `/@layer\s+[\w-]+(?:\s*,\s*[\w-]+)*\s*;/g`, so it never matches `@layer` text inside comments like `} /* end @layer zk-utilities */`) before minifying, then re-prepend at the top (allowed to precede `@import` per CSS spec) so neither the statement nor the rule after it is dropped. CSS: `js/zul/wnd/css/window.css` + `js/zul/wnd/css/panel.css` — add `white-space: nowrap` to the header so the bare title text node (inherited) can't wrap. Contract: `doc/contracts/{window,panel,groupbox}.md` — new header-*height* rows (border-box; window ≤72, panel ≤68, groupbox ≤56). Test (failing-first → passing): `screenshot.spec.ts` › `container-header-height` › window/panel/groupbox each assert `box-sizing === 'border-box'` AND a height upper bound. | Verified: 3 tests FAILED on the buggy build (`content-box`; window 88 / panel 80 / groupbox 73), PASS after the fix (window ~64, panel ~60, groupbox ~49). Full chromium suite **50/50 green — including the window & panel visual gallery baselines**, confirming the baselines already encoded the compact border-box headers and the fix restores production to match (no baseline regeneration needed). The build fix is theme-wide: every element relying on the universal reset is border-box again in production. Sibling note: groupbox 3d-mold header already used `padding:0` so it was unaffected by the box-sizing stacking; its title uses `.z-groupbox-title-content` (already nowrap) so it needed no wrap fix. |

| 2026-07-03 | combobox, bandbox, datebox | Question: clicking the INPUT text area — not just the dropdown icon — opens the popup; is that JavaScript design or a theme CSS defect? **Resolved NOT a bug, and NOT CSS.** Clicking the input opens the popup **only** for `readonly` + `buttonVisible` (intentional ZK convenience — a readonly select can't be typed into, so the whole field is the trigger). For editable it does not open on input-click (only the icon, or autodrop-on-type). A per-rule-block check can't be adopted cleanly: static CSS can't distinguish a same-element modifier (`.z-window-overlapped`, covered via the `.z-window` on the same root) from a child surface needing its own entry (`.z-datebox-popup`), so per-block scoring adds false positives (window modes, decorative `-shadow` casters, native `::picker(select)`). | impl + test (+ linter limitation logged) | CSS `zul/css/tokens/_forced-colors.css` — add `.z-datebox-popup`/`.z-daterangebox-popup` to the (1a) input-popup border list, `.z-confirmpopup` to the (1a-cont) floating-popup list, and `.z-confirmpopup-arrow` to the (1d) `forced-color-adjust:none` beak list (mirrors coachmark/errorbox); Test `src/test/playwright/forced-colors.spec.ts › datebox popup gains a border (box-shadow elevation is stripped)` + `› confirmpopup gains a border and keeps its arrow (elevation + CSS-triangle beak)`; Coverage note `doc/spec/forced-colors.md`. | Fixed & verified red→green: both tests failed pre-fix (`.z-datebox-popup` / `.z-confirmpopup` `border-top-style: none`) and pass after; full forced-colors project 13→15 green; linter 163→167 guard classes, still 0 gaps. Sibling sweep enumerated every elevation-`box-shadow` rule across `js/**/*.css` — the only real child-popup gaps were datebox/daterangebox/confirmpopup; `.z-window-*` modes (element also carries covered `.z-window`), `.z-menu*-shadow`/`.z-panel-shadow` (decorative casters; content class already covered), and `::picker(select)` (OS-rendered / already bordered) are false positives, so the linter was **not** rewritten — its per-file scoring is the residual harness gap and the Playwright assertion is the reliable gate for child-popup elevation surfaces. The pre-existing in-popup `.z-calendar` border (unchanged) merges with the new frame, so no card-within-a-card. |
| 2026-09-04 | navbar, nav, navitem | Keyboard focus on a navbar item was never styled, so Chrome's own ring stood in for it — and that ring is OUTWARD (`outline-style: auto`, 1px, rgb(0,95,204), `outline-offset: +1px`), so it was visibly **cut off**. Designer repro: expand "Get Started" → click "Step One" → press Space. Measured: the item's box (x 32 → 208.17) is exactly the box of the submenu cave `.z-nav > ul` that holds it, and that cave carries `overflow: hidden`, so the ring overshot its clip rect by 2px on every side. A second, quieter half: with no `:focus-visible` rule at all, the ring was also off-palette (UA blue, 1px) rather than `--zk-focus-ring`. | The navbar contract had no focus row of any kind — "States to evaluate" listed only default / selected / hover, so no evaluator run ever looked at focus, and a missing rule is indistinguishable from a correct one in a static screenshot (the UA draws a plausible-looking blue ring either way). Nothing in the skill said that an outward ring is unsafe on a full-bleed item; `reference/focus-affordance-no-layout-shift.md` covers focus that changes SIZE, not focus that gets CLIPPED, and its one sentence about clipping was buried in the box-shadow section. navbar also had no `components/` file at all, so the DOM facts that make the clip inevitable (unclassed cave sitting on the item's own edges, two content classes) were nowhere. | skill + contract + impl | CSS `js/zkmax/nav/css/nav.css`: `.z-nav-content:focus-visible, .z-navitem-content:focus-visible { outline: var(--zk-focus-ring); outline-offset: -2px }` — inset, same mechanism as `.z-treerow`; covers BOTH content classes because za11y's roving tabindex means arrow keys focus group headers too. CSS `zul/css/tokens/_forced-colors.css` block (2a focus): `outline-color: HighlightText` on a selected+focused navitem, because selection fill and `--zk-focus-ring` both remap to `Highlight` (measured RGB delta 78 → invisible; 687 after). Skill NEW `reference/focus-ring-clipping.md` (at-risk DOM shapes, the two working mechanisms, how to assert ring GEOMETRY not colour) + NEW `components/navbar.md` (cave/content/state-class/za11y-keyboard facts), both registered in SKILL.md. Contract `doc/contracts/navbar.md` rows c8-c11 + focus states. Tests `screenshot.spec.ts` › `navbar` › `submenu item keyboard focus ring is the theme inset ring and is not clipped` (asserts outline-style solid / 2px / primary / offset ≤ 0, and walks clipping ancestors for geometric containment) and `forced-colors.spec.ts` › `focus ring on a SELECTED navbar item stays visible (Highlight-on-Highlight)`. | Fixed & verified. Failing-first: the geometry assertion reported "ring is clipped by UL (unclassed, inside .z-nav) (overflow hidden/hidden) — ring overshoots its clip rect by 2px" plus outline-style `auto`, width 1, offset +1; all green after the fix. Forced-colors test proven RED→GREEN by temporarily removing the (2a focus) block (delta 78, threshold 200). NOT SWEPT: block (2a) fills five other selected-tint families with `Highlight` — `.z-treerow`, `.z-listitem`, `.z-paging-selected`, accordion `.z-tab-selected`, `.z-searchbox-selected`, `.z-orgitem-selected` — and any of them that draws focus with `outline` has the same Highlight-on-Highlight collision. Only navbar was measured and fixed here (blast radius of this change). `.z-treerow` is the near-certain next one (same `outline` + negative-offset mechanism); `.z-listitem` is a different pre-existing gap, since its focus marker is a `box-shadow` and forced-colors strips those outright. None of the five were measured — see the report's decision item. Also unswept: the 66 component CSS files with no `:focus-visible` rule at all (`for f in $(find src/main/resources/web/js -name '*.css'); do grep -q focus-visible $f \|\| echo $f; done`), most of them non-focusable widgets but not all. |
| 2026-09-06 | button, toolbarbutton, a, hlayout, linelayout, caption, carousel, toolbar | Follow-up sweep for the 2026-09-04 navbar row (decision D13, option A). Built a scan that finds clipped focus rings mechanically instead of waiting for a design review to report them one at a time, and ran it across every preview page. **13 distinct (element ⊂ clipper) pairs, 77 occurrences, 11 pages** — all the same shape as navbar: an OUTWARD ring (`outline-offset: 2px`) against a container that clips overflow. Worst group is `.z-button` inside `.z-hlayout` / `.z-lineitem` / `.z-caption-content` / `.z-carousel` (measured on hlayout.zul: button box 187→223, hlayout box also 187→223, so the whole 4px ring above and below is outside the clip — completely invisible, not merely cropped). Second group `.z-toolbarbutton` inside `.z-toolbar` / `.z-toolbar-popup`. Third is the preview pages' own `overflow-x: auto` state-matrix row (`overflow-x` makes the y axis compute to `auto` too, so it clips top/bottom as well). | The harness only ever measured what a contract row named, and no contract named focus geometry; the 2026-09-04 navbar fix added the rule to ONE component's contract, which by construction could not surface siblings. Nothing existed that could enumerate the population at risk. The generalisable obstacle was reachability, not measurement: driving each widget's focus by scripted Tab/click does not work uniformly (navbar's roving tabindex being the proof), so every previous attempt at breadth would have been per-component anyway. | agent/harness + doc | NEW `src/test/playwright/focus-ring-scan.spec.ts` + `focus-scan` project + `npm run test:focus-scan`. Reads the LOADED stylesheets for `:focus-visible` rules that draw an outline, forces the pseudo-class through CDP `CSS.forcePseudoState` (no interaction needed — this is what makes it general), then inflates the rect by offset+width and walks the clipping ancestors. Second pass repeats it under forced-colors for selected+focused items, hit-testing the ring band to find what the ring is really painted on. Baseline `doc/focus-ring-known-clips.json` (fails on a NEW finding and on a baseline entry that no longer reproduces, so it stays a shrinking backlog rather than a waiver list; regenerate with `FOCUS_SCAN_UPDATE=1`). Backlog + fix options written up in `tasks/focus-ring-clip-backlog.md`. | Harness verified in both directions (drop a baseline entry → "NEW clipped focus ring"; add a bogus one → "these clips are FIXED"), and the forced-colors pass verified to still go RED when the navbar (2a focus) guard is removed. 3 consecutive full runs green (55 passed / 47 skipped). NO CSS was changed by this row: the 13 pairs are recorded, not fixed, because the navbar remedy does not transfer — `--zk-focus-ring` and a filled button's fill are BOTH `--zk-color-primary`, so an inset ring on `.z-button` is primary-on-primary and would trade a clipped ring for an absent one. Needs a decision between dropping `overflow:hidden` where it is not load-bearing (only hlayout/lineitem qualify), a two-tone WCAG 2.2 focus indicator, or per-context overrides. TWO CORRECTIONS made during the run, both caught before landing: (1) the stylesheet walk found 0 rules because current Chrome exposes `cssRules` on a plain CSSStyleRule (CSS Nesting), so "has cssRules" is not "is a grouping at-rule"; (2) the forced-colors pass first reported paging as a defect by comparing the ring against the selected button's own fill — its ring is OUTSET and is painted on the bar behind the button, so it is fine. Also checked and clean: tree row, organigram node, anchornav. Noted but out of scope: accordion `.z-tab` and `.z-listitem` have NO outline focus at all (state-layer `::before` / inset `box-shadow`), both of which forced-colors strips — their focus is invisible in high contrast regardless of selection. |

No contract row asserted ring uniformity, and the evaluator reads the root's **computed** `box-shadow`, which says `inset 0 0 0 1px primary` whether or not anything paints over it — a computed value is not a visible value. | 2026-09-08 | codeeditor | First real Gate-1 measurement (46/49 PASS) found two more gaps behind the focus-ring fix. (1) **c19/c27 asserted a caret that never renders.** The CSS coloured `.cm-cursor`/`.cm-dropCursor` via `border-left-color`, but those nodes are created by CodeMirror's `drawSelection` extension and the CE build's `_baseExtensions()` does not load it — confirmed in the shipped jar and by counting nodes in a live focused editor (**0**). The rules were dead code. Measured consequence the eval report did not state: because `caret-color` does **not** inherit from `color`, the browser's native caret falls back to the UA default — pure `rgb(0,0,0)` on the light surface and `rgb(255,255,255)` on dark, against theme foregrounds `rgba(0,0,0,.87)` / `#d4d4d4` — so the caret has been off-palette the whole time and the rules intended to fix that never once applied. Two true-in-isolation statements that contradict each other, with nothing forcing them to be read together. No screenshot could catch it either: a caret is 1px wide, blinks, and a plausible-looking one renders whether or not the theme rule applies. (2) The CTV cross-cutting checklist was satisfied by *declaring* the knobs in `component-theme-variables.md`; nothing checked that a test existed, so `ctv: shipped` was recorded while the test count was zero. | skill + contract + impl + test | Skill `components/codeeditor.md` — the `.cm-cursor` bullet now states the nodes are NOT rendered, records the `caret-color`-does-not-inherit measurement, and the "legitimate styling surface" list is corrected to exclude them (the contradiction is removed, not just annotated); CSS `js/zul/code/css/codeeditor.css` — both dead rules deleted, the reason recorded under the file's existing "NOT STYLED ON PURPOSE" header so a future pass cannot re-add them; Contract `doc/contracts/codeeditor.{md,html}` — c19/c27 **retired** (ids not reused, with a note explaining the gap), mockup caret hard-coded to the UA colours it actually renders as; Test `component-theming.spec.ts` › `codeeditor — regional bg/border/gutter override…` + `› codeeditor — whole-app :root override wins…`, plus two codeeditor instances in `component-theming.zul`. | D7 = option B (clean up the dead code, accept the UA caret) and D8 = option A, both user decisions 2026-09-08. | 2026-09-09 | multislider | Every knob of a multi-range multislider is unreachable **at its own centre** — the point a user aims at. `<sliderbuttons>` is a child widget, so N ranges render N sibling `.z-sliderbuttons` divs and the theme stretches each across the full rail (`top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%`); with no `z-index` anywhere they resolve by DOM order, so the LAST range's container paints over every earlier range's knobs and swallows the pointer. Measured on `/multislider.zul`: 4 of 6 knobs dead on the 3-range horizontal slider, 2 of 4 on the 2-range vertical — `2(N-1)` of `2N`, i.e. only the topmost range is grabbable. The knob is 20px and the intercepting overlay only 4px, so the edges still respond and the widget looks and behaves fine unless you aim at the middle. | Three compounding reasons. (1) **Every preview instance that mattered had one range.** The four single-range multisliders further down the same page are genuinely healthy — the defect cannot exist below two ranges — so the only broken shape on the page was the one shape nothing asserted. (2) **No check can see it.** Contract rows, computed styles and screenshots all pass: the knob renders at the right place in the right colour, and `getComputedStyle` says nothing about who owns the pixel. (3) **The one test that did catch it named the wrong culprit.** `screenshot.spec.ts › multislider › hover` failed as `locator.hover: Timeout … <div class="z-sliderbuttons"> intercepts pointer events`, which reads as a flaky-hover/test problem and points at a rail, not at a sibling range; it had been triaged twice as a pre-existing unrelated failure (see the 2026-09-08 codeeditor row) without the mechanism being read out. | test + impl | Test NEW `src/test/playwright/hit-target.spec.ts` + `hit-target` project + `npm run test:hit-target` — asserts `elementFromPoint(knob centre) === knob` for **every** knob (not `.first()`: the topmost range always passes, so a spot check is a coin flip) across all three slider variants (multislider, rangeslider, plain slider). CSS `js/zkmax/slider/css/multislider.css` — `.z-multislider .z-sliderbuttons-button { z-index: 2 }`, scoped for the `.z-sliderbuttons-*` name collision already documented in rangeslider.css. Analysis + by-hand repro `doc/multislider-knob-hit-target-analysis.md`. | Fixed & verified RED→GREEN (guard written before the CSS): RED listed exactly 6 knobs, all naming the same blocker; GREEN after one declaration. `multislider › hover` now passes; multislider/rangeslider hover+focus and all three gallery baselines **unchanged** (the restacked elements share the accent colour); 6 CTV knob-contract tests pass; a real drag from a previously dead centre moves 70%→90% with no other knob touched. One declaration suffices because neither `.z-multislider` nor `.z-sliderbuttons` establishes a stacking context (positioned, `z-index:auto`), so containers and knobs compete in one context — `-area` deliberately gets **no** `z-index`, since knobs at 2 already outrank every area. **Two guard bugs caught before landing, both of general use for the sweep below:** (a) `elementFromPoint` takes viewport coordinates and returns `null` outside them, so every slider below the fold reported a phantom failure until each knob was `scrollIntoView`'d and its rect re-read — an unscrolled hit-test measures the fold, not the CSS; (b) disabled widgets inherit `pointer-events:none` and must be skipped via `getComputedStyle(el).pointerEvents`, not asserted. **NOT SWEPT (deferred 2026-09-09, D2 = option A, paused for the zk-repo migration):** the guard covers sliders only. The signature to hunt is narrower and more useful than "absolutely-positioned child with no z-index" — it is *a widget that renders one full-size positioned overlay per child widget*, which is what turns a missing `z-index` into a dead hit target. Such a component is invisible to every style check AND only breaks with two or more children, so the sweep needs multi-instance preview cases, not just more assertions. |
